[31m+[39m
[31m+ /**[39m
[31m+  * 前向纸面运行**专属**设置块。[39m
[31m+  *[39m
[31m+  * 为什么单独一块、而不是塞进 `realistic`：`realistic` 是与组合回测**共用**的参数容器，[39m
[31m+  * 往里加只有纸面认识的字面量，就会变成「回测参数里躺着它不消费的字段」——[39m
[31m+  * 这类漂移在审计里最难发现（D3 就是这么来的）。纸面专属的东西放在自己的命名空间下，[39m
[31m+  * 边界由类型表达，读代码的人一眼能看出「这条规则回测没有」。[39m
[31m+  *[39m
[31m+  * 缺字段 ⇒ 服务端缺省：判定时点 = 开盘+收盘，组合无条件止损 = 3%。[39m
[31m+  */[39m
[31m+ const paperTradingSettingsSchema = z.object({[39m
[31m+   /** 止损 / 动态回撤止盈的判定时点。`both` = 开盘与收盘各判一次。 */[39m
[31m+   exitJudgementPhase: z.enum(PAPER_TRADING_EXIT_PHASES).optional(),[39m
[31m+   /** 组合无条件止损阈值（%）：单票浮亏达「建仓时账户总权益」的该比例即无条件出清；0 = 关闭。 */[39m
[31m+   portfolioStopLossPercent: z.number().min(0).max(100).optional(),[39m
[31m+ });[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ /** 纸面运行参数 = 回测参数超集 + 纸面专属设置块。 */[39m
[31m+ const paperTradingOptionsSchema = backtestOptionsSchema.extend({[39m
[31m+   paperTrading: paperTradingSettingsSchema.optional(),[39m
[31m+ });[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ async function beginOperationLog([39m
[31m+   log: Parameters<typeof createOperationLog>[0][39m
[31m+ ): Promise<number | null> {[39m
[31m+   try {[39m
[31m+     return (await createOperationLog(log))?.id ?? null;[39m
[31m+   } catch (error) {[39m
[31m+     console.warn("[OperationLog] 创建日志失败，不阻断主流程:", error);[39m
[31m+     return null;[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ async function finishOperationLog([39m
[31m+   id: number | null,[39m
[31m+   changes: Parameters<typeof updateOperationLog>[1][39m
[31m+ ): Promise<void> {[39m
[31m+   if (id === null) return;[39m
[31m+   try {[39m
[31m+     await updateOperationLog(id, changes);[39m
[31m+   } catch (error) {[39m
[31m+     console.warn("[OperationLog] 更新日志失败，不阻断主流程:", error);[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ async function syncUploadedDatePrices([39m
[31m+   limitUpDate: string,[39m
[31m+   uploadedStockCodes: string[],[39m
[31m+   userId: number[39m
[31m+ ): Promise<Awaited<ReturnType<typeof syncCandidateDailyPricesForUpload>>> {[39m
[31m+   const operationLogId = await beginOperationLog({[39m
[31m+     operationType: "date_refresh",[39m
[31m+     status: "processing",[39m
[31m+     requestedDate: limitUpDate,[39m
[31m+     createdBy: userId,[39m
[31m+     message: `识别保存完成，开始按上传日期智能补全 ${limitUpDate} 的T+5行情`,[39m
[31m+   });[39m
[31m+   try {[39m
[31m+     const result = await syncCandidateDailyPricesForUpload([39m
[31m+       limitUpDate,[39m
[31m+       uploadedStockCodes[39m
[31m+     );[39m
[31m+     invalidateStockPriceSyncCache();[39m
[31m+     const allFailed =[39m
[31m+       result.targetTradingDates > 0 &&[39m
[31m+       result.failedDates.length === result.targetTradingDates &&[39m
[31m+       result.savedPriceRows === 0;[39m
[31m+     const status = allFailed[39m
[31m+       ? "failed"[39m
[31m+       : result.savedPriceRows > 0[39m
[31m+         ? "success"[39m
[31m+         : "empty";[39m
[31m+     await finishOperationLog(operationLogId, {[39m
[31m+       status,[39m
[31m+       effectiveDate: limitUpDate,[39m
[31m+       refreshedCount: result.savedPriceRows,[39m
[31m+       message: allFailed[39m
[31m+         ? `行情同步失败：${result.failedDates.join(", ")}`[39m
[31m+         : `${result.mode === "recent" ? "近期上传：补齐此前候选" : "历史上传：补齐本次图片股票"}；覆盖 ${result.signalDates.length} 个信号日及其T+5交易日，保存 ${result.savedPriceRows} 条，缺失 ${result.missingPricePairs} 条`,[39m
[31m+     });[39m
[31m+     return result;[39m
[31m+   } catch (error) {[39m
[31m+     const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);[39m
[31m+     await finishOperationLog(operationLogId, {[39m
[31m+       status: "failed",[39m
[31m+       effectiveDate: limitUpDate,[39m
[31m+       message: `行情同步失败：${message}`,[39m
[31m+     });[39m
[31m+     throw error;[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+ }[39m

[31m+     getSectorStats: publicProcedure[39m

[31m+       .input([39m
[31m+         z.object({[39m
[31m+           base64Data: z.string(),[39m

[31m+[39m

[31m+         const imageUrl = url;[39m

[31m+         // 使用 Promise 确保异步任务正常执行[39m
[31m+         void (async () => {[39m
[31m+           try {[39m
[31m+             console.log(`[uploadAndRecognize] 开始识别图片 ${imageId}...`);[39m
[31m+             console.log(`[uploadAndRecognize] 调用LLM识别图片 ${imageId}...`);[39m
[31m+             const response = await invokeLLM({[39m
[31m+               messages: [[39m
[31m+                 {[39m
[31m+                   role: "system",[39m
[31m+                   content: `你是一个专业的中文股票涨停复盘图片识别助手。请逐行提取图片中的全部涨停股票信息，并严格输出JSON。[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ 识别指南：[39m
[31m+ 1. 日期优先使用接口传入的日期；只有没有传入日期时才从图片标题识别YYYY-MM-DD。[39m
[31m+ 2. 股票代码只保留数字和交易所后缀，按市场补充.SZ、.SH或.BJ。常见代码格式：[39m
[31m+    - 深业主板: 000xxx.SZ[39m
[31m+    - 创业板: 300xxx.SZ[39m
[31m+    - 科创板: 688xxx.SH[39m
[31m+    - 北交所: 8xxxxx.BJ[39m
[31m+    - 上海主板: 600xxx.SH[39m
[31m+ 3. 股票名称：一定要是中文名称。[39m
[31m+ 4. 涨停时间：统一提取为HH:MM:SS（例如14:56:30）；只有HH:MM时补秒为:00，无法确认时返回空字符串。[39m
[31m+ 5. 板数、流通市值和成交额：保留图片中的原始值，不要猜测或改单位。[39m

[31m+[39m
[31m+ 如果图片中不清楚或没有某个字段：日期使用提供的日期参数，其他字段使用空字符串；不要编造股票代码、名称或数值。[39m

[31m+ {[39m
[31m+   "date": "涨停日期，格式为YYYY-MM-DD",[39m
[31m+   "stocks": [[39m

[31m+ 3. 股票代码、名称、题材和关键词必须是中文格式[39m
[31m+ 4. 返回的JSON中stocks数组不能为空（除非图片中确实没有任何股票信息）[39m
[31m+ 5. 如果图片中有表格，请逐行提取每一只股票[39m
[31m+ 6. 如果股票名称后面有代码，代码格式应该是 XXX.SZ 或 XXX.SH 这样的格式`,[39m
[31m+                     },[39m
[31m+                     {[39m
[31m+                       type: "image_url",[39m
[31m+                       image_url: {[39m

[31m+                         detail: "high",[39m

[31m+                   ],[39m

[31m+               response_format: {[39m

[31m+                             limitUpTime: { type: "string" },[39m
[31m+                             boardCount: { type: "string" },[39m
[31m+                             circulationValue: { type: "string" },[39m

[31m+             const rawContent = response.choices[0]?.message?.content;[39m

[31m+             await updateImageStatus(imageId, "completed");[39m
[31m+             await finishOperationLog(operationLogId, {[39m
[31m+               status: stocks.length > 0 ? "success" : "empty",[39m
[31m+               effectiveDate: recognizedDate,[39m
[31m+               recognizedCount: stocks.length,[39m
[31m+               message:[39m
[31m+                 stocks.length > 0[39m
[31m+                   ? `识别并保存 ${stocks.length} 条股票记录`[39m
[31m+                   : "图片中未识别到可保存的股票记录",[39m
[31m+             });[39m
[31m+             if (stocks.length > 0) {[39m
[31m+               try {[39m
[31m+                 const marketSync = await syncUploadedDatePrices([39m

[31m+                   userId[39m
[31m+                 );[39m
[31m+                 console.log([39m
[31m+                   `[uploadAndRecognize] 图片 ${imageId} 行情同步完成，保存 ${marketSync.savedPriceRows} 条价格记录`[39m
[31m+                 );[39m
[31m+               } catch (syncError) {[39m
[31m+                 console.error([39m
[31m+                   `[uploadAndRecognize] 图片 ${imageId} 行情同步失败：`,[39m

[31m+                 );[39m
[31m+               }[39m
[31m+             }[39m
[31m+             console.log([39m

[31m+           } catch (error) {[39m

[31m+               await updateImageStatus(imageId, "failed");[39m
[31m+             } catch (updateError) {[39m
[31m+               console.error([39m

[31m+                 updateError[39m
[31m+               );[39m
[31m+             }[39m
[31m+           }[39m
[31m+         })();[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         // 立即返回成功，不等待识别完成[39m
[31m+         return {[39m
[31m+           success: true,[39m

[31m+   // 图片识别与日期数据刷新操作日志[39m
[31m+   operationLog: router({[39m
[31m+     getRecent: protectedProcedure[39m
[31m+       .input([39m
[31m+         z[39m
[31m+           .object({[39m
[31m+             operationType: z[39m
[31m+               .enum(["image_recognition", "date_refresh"])[39m
[31m+               .optional(),[39m
[31m+             status: z[39m
[31m+               .enum(["processing", "success", "empty", "failed"])[39m
[31m+               .optional(),[39m
[31m+             date: z[39m
[31m+               .string()[39m
[31m+               .regex(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/)[39m
[31m+               .optional(),[39m

[31m+           })[39m
[31m+           .optional()[39m
[31m+       )[39m
[31m+       .query(async ({ ctx, input }) => {[39m
[31m+         return await getOperationLogs(ctx.user.id, input);[39m

[31m+     recordRefresh: protectedProcedure[39m

[31m+           message: z.string().max(2000).optional(),[39m
[31m+         })[39m

[31m+       .mutation(async ({ ctx, input }) => {[39m
[31m+         const log = await createOperationLog({[39m
[31m+           operationType: "date_refresh",[39m
[31m+           status: input.status,[39m
[31m+           requestedDate: input.date,[39m

[31m+           message: input.message ?? null,[39m
[31m+           createdBy: ctx.user.id,[39m
[31m+         });[39m
[31m+         if (!log) {[39m
[31m+           throw new TRPCError({[39m
[31m+             code: "INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR",[39m
[31m+             message: "操作日志保存失败",[39m
[31m+           });[39m

[31m+       }),[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     retry: protectedProcedure[39m
[31m+       .input(z.object({ logId: z.number().int().positive() }))[39m
[31m+       .mutation(async ({ ctx, input }) => {[39m
[31m+         const sourceLog = await getOperationLogById(input.logId, ctx.user.id);[39m
[31m+         if (!sourceLog) {[39m
[31m+           throw new TRPCError({[39m
[31m+             code: "NOT_FOUND",[39m
[31m+             message: "操作日志不存在或无权访问",[39m
[31m+           });[39m
[31m+         }[39m
[31m+         if (sourceLog.status !== "failed") {[39m
[31m+           throw new TRPCError({[39m
[31m+             code: "BAD_REQUEST",[39m
[31m+             message: "只有失败状态的操作可以重试",[39m
[31m+           });[39m
[31m+         }[39m
[31m+         if (!sourceLog.requestedDate) {[39m
[31m+           throw new TRPCError({[39m
[31m+             code: "BAD_REQUEST",[39m
[31m+             message: "该日志缺少原始日期，无法重试",[39m

[31m+               code: "BAD_REQUEST",[39m

[31m+             });[39m

[31m+             operationType: sourceLog.operationType,[39m

[31m+             status: "ready" as const,[39m
[31m+             retryInput: {[39m

[31m+           const status = records.length > 0 ? "success" : "empty";[39m
[31m+           const retryLog = await createOperationLog({[39m
[31m+             operationType: "date_refresh",[39m
[31m+             status,[39m
[31m+             requestedDate: sourceLog.requestedDate,[39m
[31m+             effectiveDate: sourceLog.requestedDate,[39m
[31m+             refreshedCount: records.length,[39m

[31m+             sourceLogId: sourceLog.id,[39m
[31m+             retryLogId: retryLog.id,[39m
[31m+             status,[39m
[31m+             count: records.length,[39m
[31m+             message: retryLog.message,[39m
[31m+           };[39m
[31m+         } catch (error) {[39m

[31m+           });[39m
[31m+           if (!retryLog) {[39m
[31m+             throw error;[39m
[31m+           }[39m
[31m+           return {[39m
[31m+             operationType: sourceLog.operationType,[39m
[31m+             sourceLogId: sourceLog.id,[39m
[31m+             retryLogId: retryLog.id,[39m
[31m+             status: "failed" as const,[39m
[31m+             count: 0,[39m

[31m+ // 建运行前置校验的领域错误（日历落后）；领域码必须经 message 跨过 tRPC 边界。[39m
[31m+ import { PaperTradingCalendarStaleError, PAPER_TRADING_EXIT_PHASES } from "./paperTrading";[39m

[31m+ const limitUpTimeInput = z.string().refine(isValidLimitUpTime, {[39m
[31m+   message: "涨停时间应为HH:MM或HH:MM:SS格式",[39m
[31m+ });[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ /** 次日开盘预期三档：期望档位表（center/lower/upper，单位 %）。 */[39m
[31m+ const expectationTableSchema = z.object({[39m
[31m+   early: z.object({ center: z.number(), lower: z.number(), upper: z.number() }),[39m
[31m+   morning: z.object({[39m
[31m+     center: z.number(),[39m
[31m+     lower: z.number(),[39m
[31m+     upper: z.number(),[39m
[31m+   }),[39m
[31m+   afternoon: z.object({[39m
[31m+     center: z.number(),[39m
[31m+     lower: z.number(),[39m
[31m+     upper: z.number(),[39m
[31m+   }),[39m
[31m+   late: z.object({ center: z.number(), lower: z.number(), upper: z.number() }),[39m
[31m+   unknown: z.object({[39m
[31m+     center: z.number(),[39m
[31m+     lower: z.number(),[39m
[31m+     upper: z.number(),[39m
[31m+   }),[39m
[31m+ });[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ /** 情绪周期阶段 schema：与 server/sentimentCycle.ts 的阶段常量同源，避免字面量漂移。 */[39m
[31m+ const sentimentCyclePhaseSchema = z.enum(SENTIMENT_CYCLE_PHASES);[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ /** 回测参数 schema：计算与保存共用，保证保存的参数能被直接复算。 */[39m
[31m+ const backtestOptionsSchema = z.object({[39m
[31m+   startDate: z.string().regex(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/).optional(),[39m
[31m+   endDate: z.string().regex(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/).optional(),[39m
[31m+   observationDays: z.union([z.literal(1), z.literal(2)]).default(1),[39m
[31m+   minScore: z.number().int().min(0).max(100).optional(),[39m
[31m+   realistic: z[39m
[31m+     .object({[39m
[31m+       initialCapital: z.number().positive().max(100000000).optional(),[39m
[31m+       maxPositions: z.number().int().min(1).max(100).optional(),[39m
[31m+       commissionRate: z.number().min(0).max(0.01).optional(),[39m
[31m+       stampDutyRate: z.number().min(0).max(0.01).optional(),[39m
[31m+       transferFeeRate: z.number().min(0).max(0.01).optional(),[39m
[31m+       slippageBps: z.number().min(0).max(1000).optional(),[39m
[31m+       lotSize: z.number().int().min(1).max(10000).optional(),[39m
[31m+       blockLimitUpBuys: z.boolean().optional(),[39m
[31m+       blockLimitDownSells: z.boolean().optional(),[39m
[31m+       enableOneWordLimitDownProbability: z.boolean().optional(),[39m
[31m+       oneWordLimitDownSellProbability: z.number().min(0).max(100).optional(),[39m
[31m+       positionSizingStrategy: z[39m
[31m+         .enum(["equal", "scoreWeighted", "fixedPercent"])[39m
[31m+         .optional(),[39m
[31m+       fixedPositionPercent: z.number().min(1).max(100).optional(),[39m
[31m+       trailingProfitActivationPercent: z.number().min(0).max(100).optional(),[39m
[31m+       trailingDrawdownPercent: z.number().min(0).max(100).optional(),[39m
[31m+       stopLossPercent: z.number().min(0).max(100).optional(),[39m
[31m+       strongHoldMinReturn: z.number().min(0).max(100).optional(),[39m
[31m+       maxHoldingDays: z.number().int().min(2).max(30).optional(),[39m
[31m+       minimumExpectedOpenChangePercent: z.number().min(-50).max(100).optional(),[39m
[31m+       expectationTierEnabled: z.boolean().optional(),[39m
[31m+       expectationTable: expectationTableSchema.optional(),[39m
[31m+       blockOneWordLimitUpBuys: z.boolean().optional(),[39m
[31m+       enableIntradayStopLoss: z.boolean().optional(),[39m
[31m+       detectExRights: z.boolean().optional(),[39m
[31m+       maxPositionAmountRatio: z.number().min(0).max(1).optional(),[39m
[31m+     })[39m
[31m+     .optional(),[39m
[31m+   downsideRisk: z[39m
[31m+     .object({[39m
[31m+       observationDays: z.number().int().min(2).max(10).optional(),[39m
[31m+       mediumDownsidePercent: z.number().min(1).max(50).optional(),[39m
[31m+       highDownsidePercent: z.number().min(1).max(50).optional(),[39m
[31m+       penaltyWeight: z.number().min(0).max(1).optional(),[39m
[31m+       autoTunePenaltyWeight: z.boolean().optional(),[39m
[31m+       hardRiskThreshold: z.number().min(0).max(100).optional(),[39m
[31m+       rollingTrainTradingDays: z.number().int().min(30).max(150).optional(),[39m
[31m+       rollingValidationTradingDays: z.number().int().min(10).max(60).optional(),[39m
[31m+       /** 高位连板风控：允许参与的最高连板高度（超过即限制参与；5/6 板降低仓位）。 */[39m
[31m+       maxParticipatingBoards: z.number().int().min(1).max(20).optional(),[39m
[31m+     })[39m
[31m+     .optional(),[39m
[31m+ });[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ /**[39m
[31m+  * 前向纸面运行**专属**设置块。[39m
[31m+  *[39m
[31m+  * 为什么单独一块、而不是塞进 `realistic`：`realistic` 是与组合回测**共用**的参数容器，[39m
[31m+  * 往里加只有纸面认识的字面量，就会变成「回测参数里躺着它不消费的字段」——[39m
[31m+  * 这类漂移在审计里最难发现（D3 就是这么来的）。纸面专属的东西放在自己的命名空间下，[39m
[31m+  * 边界由类型表达，读代码的人一眼能看出「这条规则回测没有」。[39m
[31m+  *[39m
[31m+  * 缺字段 ⇒ 服务端缺省：判定时点 = 开盘+收盘，组合无条件止损 = 3%。[39m
[31m+  */[39m
[31m+ const paperTradingSettingsSchema = z.object({[39m
[31m+   /** 止损 / 动态回撤止盈的判定时点。`both` = 开盘与收盘各判一次。 */[39m
[31m+   exitJudgementPhase: z.enum(PAPER_TRADING_EXIT_PHASES).optional(),[39m
[31m+   /** 组合无条件止损阈值（%）：单票浮亏达「建仓时账户总权益」的该比例即无条件出清；0 = 关闭。 */[39m
[31m+   portfolioStopLossPercent: z.number().min(0).max(100).optional(),[39m
[31m+ });[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ /** 纸面运行参数 = 回测参数超集 + 纸面专属设置块。 */[39m
[31m+ const paperTradingOptionsSchema = backtestOptionsSchema.extend({[39m
[31m+   paperTrading: paperTradingSettingsSchema.optional(),[39m
[31m+ });[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ async function beginOperationLog([39m
[31m+   log: Parameters<typeof createOperationLog>[0][39m
[31m+ ): Promise<number | null> {[39m
[31m+   try {[39m
[31m+     return (await createOperationLog(log))?.id ?? null;[39m
[31m+   } catch (error) {[39m
[31m+     console.warn("[OperationLog] 创建日志失败，不阻断主流程:", error);[39m
[31m+     return null;[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ async function finishOperationLog([39m
[31m+   id: number | null,[39m
[31m+   changes: Parameters<typeof updateOperationLog>[1][39m
[31m+ ): Promise<void> {[39m
[31m+   if (id === null) return;[39m
[31m+   try {[39m
[31m+     await updateOperationLog(id, changes);[39m
[31m+   } catch (error) {[39m
[31m+     console.warn("[OperationLog] 更新日志失败，不阻断主流程:", error);[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ async function syncUploadedDatePrices([39m
[31m+   limitUpDate: string,[39m
[31m+   uploadedStockCodes: string[],[39m
[31m+   userId: number[39m
[31m+ ): Promise<Awaited<ReturnType<typeof syncCandidateDailyPricesForUpload>>> {[39m
[31m+   const operationLogId = await beginOperationLog({[39m
[31m+     operationType: "date_refresh",[39m
[31m+     status: "processing",[39m
[31m+     requestedDate: limitUpDate,[39m
[31m+     createdBy: userId,[39m
[31m+     message: `识别保存完成，开始按上传日期智能补全 ${limitUpDate} 的T+5行情`,[39m
[31m+   });[39m
[31m+   try {[39m
[31m+     const result = await syncCandidateDailyPricesForUpload([39m
[31m+       limitUpDate,[39m
[31m+       uploadedStockCodes[39m
[31m+     );[39m
[31m+     invalidateStockPriceSyncCache();[39m
[31m+     const allFailed =[39m
[31m+       result.targetTradingDates > 0 &&[39m
[31m+       result.failedDates.length === result.targetTradingDates &&[39m
[31m+       result.savedPriceRows === 0;[39m
[31m+     const status = allFailed[39m
[31m+       ? "failed"[39m
[31m+       : result.savedPriceRows > 0[39m
[31m+         ? "success"[39m
[31m+         : "empty";[39m
[31m+     await finishOperationLog(operationLogId, {[39m
[31m+       status,[39m
[31m+       effectiveDate: limitUpDate,[39m
[31m+       refreshedCount: result.savedPriceRows,[39m
[31m+       message: allFailed[39m
[31m+         ? `行情同步失败：${result.failedDates.join(", ")}`[39m
[31m+         : `${result.mode === "recent" ? "近期上传：补齐此前候选" : "历史上传：补齐本次图片股票"}；覆盖 ${result.signalDates.length} 个信号日及其T+5交易日，保存 ${result.savedPriceRows} 条，缺失 ${result.missingPricePairs} 条`,[39m
[31m+     });[39m
[31m+     return result;[39m
[31m+   } catch (error) {[39m
[31m+     const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);[39m
[31m+     await finishOperationLog(operationLogId, {[39m
[31m+       status: "failed",[39m
[31m+       effectiveDate: limitUpDate,[39m
[31m+       message: `行情同步失败：${message}`,[39m
[31m+     });[39m
[31m+     throw error;[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+ }[39m

[31m+ export const appRouter = router({[39m

[31m+   researchPlanner: researchPlannerRouter,[39m

[31m+   researchRun: researchRunRouter,[39m
[31m+   // FE-1 — 数据域健康看板[39m
[31m+   dataHealth: dataHealthRouter,[39m
[31m+   // FE-6/7/8/9 — 研究量化链路（技术预览口径，真实数据注入见各 router 头注释）[39m
[31m+   paramSearch: paramSearchRouter,[39m

[31m+   auth: router({[39m
[31m+     me: publicProcedure.query(opts => opts.ctx.user),[39m
[31m+     logout: publicProcedure.mutation(({ ctx }) => {[39m
[31m+       const cookieOptions = getSessionCookieOptions(ctx.req);[39m
[31m+       ctx.res.clearCookie(COOKIE_NAME, { ...cookieOptions, maxAge: -1 });[39m
[31m+       return { success: true } as const;[39m
[31m+     }),[39m
[31m+   }),[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   // 涨停记录相关API[39m
[31m+   limitUp: router({[39m
[31m+     // 获取所有涨停记录[39m
[31m+     getAll: publicProcedure.query(async () => {[39m
[31m+       return await getAllLimitUpRecords();[39m
[31m+     }),[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     // 按日期获取涨停记录[39m
[31m+     getByDate: publicProcedure[39m
[31m+       .input(z.object({ date: z.string() }))[39m
[31m+       .query(async ({ input }) => {[39m
[31m+         return await getLimitUpRecordsByDate(input.date);[39m
[31m+       }),[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     // 搜索股票[39m
[31m+     search: publicProcedure[39m
[31m+       .input(z.object({ query: z.string() }))[39m
[31m+       .query(async ({ input }) => {[39m
[31m+         if (!input.query.trim()) return [];[39m
[31m+         return await searchLimitUpRecords(input.query);[39m
[31m+       }),[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     // 获取每日题材统计[39m
[31m+     getSectorStats: publicProcedure[39m
[31m+       .input(z.object({ date: z.string() }))[39m
[31m+       .query(async ({ input }) => {[39m
[31m+         return await getDailySectorStats(input.date);[39m
[31m+       }),[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     // 获取所有日期列表[39m
[31m+     getDates: publicProcedure.query(async () => {[39m
[31m+       return await getDistinctDates();[39m
[31m+     }),[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     // 获取每日涨停数量统计[39m
[31m+     getDailyStats: publicProcedure.query(async () => {[39m
[31m+       return await getDailyLimitUpStats();[39m
[31m+     }),[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     // 获取每日题材分布统计[39m
[31m+     getSectorDistribution: publicProcedure.query(async () => {[39m
[31m+       return await getDailySectorDistribution();[39m
[31m+     }),[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     // 创建涨停记录[39m
[31m+     create: protectedProcedure[39m
[31m+       .input([39m
[31m+         z.object({[39m
[31m+           stockCode: z.string(),[39m
[31m+           stockName: z.string(),[39m
[31m+           limitUpDate: z.string(),[39m
[31m+           limitUpTime: limitUpTimeInput.optional(),[39m
[31m+           boardCount: z.string().optional(),[39m
[31m+           circulationValue: z.string().optional(),[39m
[31m+           turnover: z.string().optional(),[39m
[31m+           sector: z.string().optional(),[39m
[31m+           keywords: z.string().optional(),[39m
[31m+         })[39m
[31m+       )[39m
[31m+       .mutation(async ({ input, ctx }) => {[39m
[31m+         // 直接使用字符串格式日期，避免时区转换[39m
[31m+         return await createLimitUpRecord({[39m
[31m+           ...input,[39m
[31m+           limitUpDate: input.limitUpDate,[39m
[31m+           createdBy: ctx.user.id,[39m
[31m+         });[39m
[31m+       }),[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     // 更新涨停记录[39m
[31m+     update: protectedProcedure[39m
[31m+       .input([39m
[31m+         z.object({[39m
[31m+           id: z.number(),[39m
[31m+           stockCode: z.string().optional(),[39m
[31m+           stockName: z.string().optional(),[39m
[31m+           limitUpDate: z.string().optional(),[39m
[31m+           limitUpTime: limitUpTimeInput.optional(),[39m
[31m+           boardCount: z.string().optional(),[39m
[31m+           circulationValue: z.string().optional(),[39m
[31m+           turnover: z.string().optional(),[39m
[31m+           sector: z.string().optional(),[39m
[31m+           keywords: z.string().optional(),[39m
[31m+         })[39m
[31m+       )[39m
[31m+       .mutation(async ({ input }) => {[39m
[31m+         const { id, ...data } = input;[39m
[31m+         const updateData: Record<string, unknown> = { ...data };[39m
[31m+         if (data.limitUpDate) {[39m
[31m+           updateData.limitUpDate = new Date(data.limitUpDate);[39m
[31m+         }[39m
[31m+         return await updateLimitUpRecord(id, updateData);[39m
[31m+       }),[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     // 删除涨停记录[39m
[31m+     delete: protectedProcedure[39m
[31m+       .input(z.object({ id: z.number() }))[39m
[31m+       .mutation(async ({ input }) => {[39m
[31m+         return await deleteLimitUpRecord(input.id);[39m
[31m+       }),[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     // 获取股票关注状态[39m
[31m+     getWatchStatus: protectedProcedure[39m
[31m+       .input(z.object({ stockCode: z.string() }))[39m
[31m+       .query(async ({ ctx, input }) => {[39m
[31m+         const watched = await isStockWatched(ctx.user.id, input.stockCode);[39m
[31m+         if (!watched) return "none";[39m
[31m+         return watched.watchType;[39m
[31m+       }),[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     // 更新股票关注状态[39m
[31m+     updateWatchStatus: protectedProcedure[39m
[31m+       .input([39m
[31m+         z.object({[39m
[31m+           stockCode: z.string(),[39m
[31m+           stockName: z.string(),[39m
[31m+           watchStatus: z.enum(["none", "normal", "important"]),[39m
[31m+         })[39m
[31m+       )[39m
[31m+       .mutation(async ({ ctx, input }) => {[39m
[31m+         if (input.watchStatus === "none") {[39m
[31m+           return await removeFromWatchlist(ctx.user.id, input.stockCode);[39m
[31m+         } else {[39m
[31m+           const existing = await isStockWatched(ctx.user.id, input.stockCode);[39m
[31m+           if (existing) {[39m
[31m+             return await updateWatchType([39m
[31m+               ctx.user.id,[39m
[31m+               input.stockCode,[39m
[31m+               input.watchStatus[39m
[31m+             );[39m
[31m+           } else {[39m
[31m+             return await addToWatchlist([39m
[31m+               ctx.user.id,[39m
[31m+               input.stockCode,[39m
[31m+               input.stockName,[39m
[31m+               input.watchStatus[39m
[31m+             );[39m
[31m+           }[39m
[31m+         }[39m
[31m+       }),[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     // 获取连板梯队统计[39m
[31m+     getConnectionBoardStats: publicProcedure[39m
[31m+       .input(z.object({ date: z.string() }))[39m
[31m+       .query(async ({ input }) => {[39m
[31m+         return await getConnectionBoardStats(input.date);[39m
[31m+       }),[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     // MARKET-PAGE-REWORK —— 连板梯队名录（连板股 + 断板股），大盘分析页「连板梯队」大表格用。[39m
[31m+     // 与上行 getConnectionBoardStats 的区别只在**取数范围**：这里照窗口取（默认 60 自然日），[39m
[31m+     // 不再全表 select（实测 68.3s）；连板判定规则与情绪评分公式与既有实现完全一致。[39m
[31m+     getBoardRoster: publicProcedure[39m

[31m+             );[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+             if (stocks.length > 0) {[39m
[31m+               const records = stocks.map((stock: any) => ({[39m
[31m+                 stockCode: stock.stockCode,[39m
[31m+                 stockName: stock.stockName,[39m
[31m+                 limitUpDate: recognizedDate,[39m
[31m+                 limitUpTime: stock.limitUpTime || null,[39m
[31m+                 boardCount: stock.boardCount || null,[39m
[31m+                 circulationValue: stock.circulationValue || null,[39m
[31m+                 turnover: stock.turnover || null,[39m
[31m+                 sector: stock.sector || null,[39m
[31m+                 keywords: stock.keywords || null,[39m
[31m+                 createdBy: userId,[39m
[31m+               }));[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+               console.log([39m
[31m+                 `[uploadAndRecognize] 保存 ${records.length} 条股票记录到数据库...`[39m
[31m+               );[39m
[31m+               await createLimitUpRecordsBatch(records);[39m
[31m+               console.log(`[uploadAndRecognize] 股票记录保存成功`);[39m
[31m+             }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+             await updateImageStatus(imageId, "completed");[39m
[31m+             await finishOperationLog(operationLogId, {[39m
[31m+               status: stocks.length > 0 ? "success" : "empty",[39m
[31m+               effectiveDate: recognizedDate,[39m
[31m+               recognizedCount: stocks.length,[39m
[31m+               message:[39m
[31m+                 stocks.length > 0[39m
[31m+                   ? `识别并保存 ${stocks.length} 条股票记录`[39m
[31m+                   : "图片中未识别到可保存的股票记录",[39m
[31m+             });[39m
[31m+             if (stocks.length > 0) {[39m
[31m+               try {[39m
[31m+                 const marketSync = await syncUploadedDatePrices([39m
[31m+                   recognizedDate,[39m
[31m+                   stocks.map(stock => stock.stockCode),[39m
[31m+                   userId[39m
[31m+                 );[39m
[31m+                 console.log([39m
[31m+                   `[uploadAndRecognize] 图片 ${imageId} 行情同步完成，保存 ${marketSync.savedPriceRows} 条价格记录`[39m
[31m+                 );[39m
[31m+               } catch (syncError) {[39m
[31m+                 console.error([39m
[31m+                   `[uploadAndRecognize] 图片 ${imageId} 行情同步失败：`,[39m
[31m+                   syncError[39m
[31m+                 );[39m
[31m+               }[39m
[31m+             }[39m
[31m+             console.log([39m
[31m+               `[uploadAndRecognize] 图片 ${imageId} 识别完成，识别出 ${stocks.length} 只股票`[39m
[31m+             );[39m
[31m+           } catch (error) {[39m
[31m+             const errorMessage =[39m
[31m+               error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);[39m
[31m+             console.error([39m
[31m+               "[uploadAndRecognize] 识别失败:",[39m
[31m+               errorMessage,[39m
[31m+               error instanceof Error ? error.stack : ""[39m
[31m+             );[39m
[31m+             await finishOperationLog(operationLogId, {[39m
[31m+               status: "failed",[39m
[31m+               message: errorMessage,[39m
[31m+             });[39m
[31m+             try {[39m
[31m+               await updateImageStatus(imageId, "failed");[39m
[31m+             } catch (updateError) {[39m
[31m+               console.error([39m
[31m+                 "[uploadAndRecognize] 更新图片状态失败:",[39m
[31m+                 updateError[39m
[31m+               );[39m
[31m+             }[39m
[31m+           }[39m
[31m+         })();[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         // 立即返回成功，不等待识别完成[39m
[31m+         return {[39m
[31m+           success: true,[39m
[31m+           imageId: image.id,[39m
[31m+           message: "图片上传成功，正在后台识别中...",[39m
[31m+         };[39m
[31m+       }),[39m
[31m+   }),[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   // 图片识别与日期数据刷新操作日志[39m
[31m+   operationLog: router({[39m
[31m+     getRecent: protectedProcedure[39m
[31m+       .input([39m
[31m+         z[39m
[31m+           .object({[39m
[31m+             operationType: z[39m
[31m+               .enum(["image_recognition", "date_refresh"])[39m
[31m+               .optional(),[39m
[31m+             status: z[39m
[31m+               .enum(["processing", "success", "empty", "failed"])[39m
[31m+               .optional(),[39m
[31m+             date: z[39m
[31m+               .string()[39m
[31m+               .regex(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/)[39m
[31m+               .optional(),[39m
[31m+             limit: z.number().int().min(1).max(200).optional(),[39m
[31m+           })[39m
[31m+           .optional()[39m
[31m+       )[39m
[31m+       .query(async ({ ctx, input }) => {[39m
[31m+         return await getOperationLogs(ctx.user.id, input);[39m
[31m+       }),[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     recordRefresh: protectedProcedure[39m
[31m+       .input([39m
[31m+         z.object({[39m
[31m+           date: z.string().regex(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/),[39m
[31m+           status: z.enum(["success", "empty", "failed"]),[39m
[31m+           refreshedCount: z.number().int().min(0).optional(),[39m
[31m+           message: z.string().max(2000).optional(),[39m
[31m+         })[39m
[31m+       )[39m
[31m+       .mutation(async ({ ctx, input }) => {[39m
[31m+         const log = await createOperationLog({[39m
[31m+           operationType: "date_refresh",[39m
[31m+           status: input.status,[39m
[31m+           requestedDate: input.date,[39m
[31m+           effectiveDate: input.date,[39m
[31m+           refreshedCount: input.refreshedCount ?? null,[39m
[31m+           message: input.message ?? null,[39m
[31m+           createdBy: ctx.user.id,[39m
[31m+         });[39m
[31m+         if (!log) {[39m
[31m+           throw new TRPCError({[39m
[31m+             code: "INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR",[39m
[31m+             message: "操作日志保存失败",[39m
[31m+           });[39m
[31m+         }[39m
[31m+         return log;[39m
[31m+       }),[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     retry: protectedProcedure[39m
[31m+       .input(z.object({ logId: z.number().int().positive() }))[39m
[31m+       .mutation(async ({ ctx, input }) => {[39m
[31m+         const sourceLog = await getOperationLogById(input.logId, ctx.user.id);[39m
[31m+         if (!sourceLog) {[39m
[31m+           throw new TRPCError({[39m
[31m+             code: "NOT_FOUND",[39m
[31m+             message: "操作日志不存在或无权访问",[39m
[31m+           });[39m
[31m+         }[39m
[31m+         if (sourceLog.status !== "failed") {[39m
[31m+           throw new TRPCError({[39m
[31m+             code: "BAD_REQUEST",[39m
[31m+             message: "只有失败状态的操作可以重试",[39m
[31m+           });[39m
[31m+         }[39m
[31m+         if (!sourceLog.requestedDate) {[39m
[31m+           throw new TRPCError({[39m
[31m+             code: "BAD_REQUEST",[39m
[31m+             message: "该日志缺少原始日期，无法重试",[39m
[31m+           });[39m
[31m+         }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         if (sourceLog.operationType === "image_recognition") {[39m
[31m+           if (!sourceLog.imageUrl) {[39m
[31m+             throw new TRPCError({[39m
[31m+               code: "BAD_REQUEST",[39m
[31m+               message: "该失败日志缺少原始图片，无法重试",[39m
[31m+             });[39m
[31m+           }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+           return {[39m
[31m+             operationType: sourceLog.operationType,[39m
[31m+             sourceLogId: sourceLog.id,[39m
[31m+             retryLogId: null,[39m
[31m+             status: "ready" as const,[39m
[31m+             retryInput: {[39m
[31m+               imageUrl: sourceLog.imageUrl,[39m
[31m+               imageId: sourceLog.imageId ?? undefined,[39m
[31m+               fileName: sourceLog.fileName ?? undefined,[39m
[31m+               limitUpDate: sourceLog.requestedDate,[39m
[31m+             },[39m
[31m+             message: "已校验原始图片和日期，正在重新执行识别",[39m
[31m+           };[39m
[31m+         }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+         try {[39m
[31m+           const records = await getLimitUpRecordsByDate([39m
[31m+             sourceLog.requestedDate[39m
[31m+           );[39m
[31m+           const status = records.length > 0 ? "success" : "empty";[39m
[31m+           const retryLog = await createOperationLog({[39m
[31m+             operationType: "date_refresh",[39m
[31m+             status,[39m
[31m+             requestedDate: sourceLog.requestedDate,[39m
[31m+             effectiveDate: sourceLog.requestedDate,[39m
[31m+             refreshedCount: records.length,[39m
[31m+             message:[39m
[31m+               status === "success"[39m
[31m+                 ? `重试刷新到 ${records.length} 条记录`[39m
[31m+                 : "重试刷新成功但没有记录",[39m
[31m+             createdBy: ctx.user.id,[39m
[31m+           });[39m
[31m+           if (!retryLog) {[39m
[31m+             throw new Error("重试日志保存失败");[39m
[31m+           }[39m
[31m+           return {[39m
[31m+             operationType: sourceLog.operationType,[39m
[31m+             sourceLogId: sourceLog.id,[39m
[31m+             retryLogId: retryLog.id,[39m
[31m+             status,[39m
[31m+             count: records.length,[39m
[31m+             message: retryLog.message,[39m
[31m+           };[39m
[31m+         } catch (error) {[39m
[31m+           const retryLog = await createOperationLog({[39m
[31m+             operationType: "date_refresh",[39m
[31m+             status: "failed",[39m
[31m+             requestedDate: sourceLog.requestedDate,[39m
[31m+             effectiveDate: sourceLog.requestedDate,[39m
[31m+             refreshedCount: 0,[39m
[31m+             message:[39m
[31m+               error instanceof Error[39m
[31m+                 ? `重试失败：${error.message}`[39m
[31m+                 : "重试失败",[39m
[31m+             createdBy: ctx.user.id,[39m
[31m+           });[39m
[31m+           if (!retryLog) {[39m
[31m+             throw error;[39m
[31m+           }[39m
[31m+           return {[39m
[31m+             operationType: sourceLog.operationType,[39m
[31m+             sourceLogId: sourceLog.id,[39m
[31m+             retryLogId: retryLog.id,[39m
[31m+             status: "failed" as const,[39m
[31m+             count: 0,[39m
[31m+             message: retryLog.message,[39m
[31m+           };[39m
[31m+         }[39m
[31m+       }),[39m
[31m+   }),[39m

[31m+   // 大盘数据相关API[39m

[31m+   // 情绪预警相关API[39m
[31m+   sentiment: router({[39m
[31m+     // 获取可解释的主板龙头候选池（收盘后复盘排序，不构成交易建议）[39m
[31m+     getLeaderCandidates: publicProcedure.query(async () => {[39m
[31m+       return await getLeaderCandidates();[39m
[31m+     }),[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     // 按历史候选池计算下一已记录交易日的连板延续结果（聚合结果 + 明细行数）。[39m
[31m+     // 2026-09-11 性能修复：不再随响应回传全量 historicalRows（数万行 / 数十 MB），[39m
[31m+     // 明细改由 getLeaderCandidateHistoryPage 服务端分页获取；聚合口径未变。[39m
[31m+     getLeaderCandidateBacktest: publicProcedure[39m
[31m+       .input(backtestOptionsSchema.optional())[39m
[31m+       .query(async ({ input }) => {[39m
[31m+         // STEP 5 P2-2：生产核心路径 —— 不执行 research-legacy 模拟器。[39m
[31m+         return stripLeaderCandidateHistory(await getLeaderCandidateBacktest(input));[39m
[31m+       }),[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     // 全样本历史明细分页（服务端过滤 + 切片，单次响应恒 <= pageSize 行）。[39m
[31m+     // 复用同一份回测结果缓存，因此翻页不会重复跑全量模拟。[39m
[31m+     getLeaderCandidateHistoryPage: publicProcedure[39m
[31m+       .input([39m
[31m+         backtestOptionsSchema.extend({[39m
[31m+           page: z.number().int().min(1).optional(),[39m
[31m+           pageSize: z.number().int().min(1).max(MAX_LEADER_CANDIDATE_HISTORY_PAGE_SIZE).optional(),[39m
[31m+           phase: sentimentCyclePhaseSchema.nullish(),[39m
[31m+           onlySuccess: z.boolean().nullish(),[39m
[31m+         }).optional(),[39m
[31m+       )[39m
[31m+       .query(async ({ input }) => {[39m
[31m+         const { page, pageSize, phase, onlySuccess, ...backtestOptions } = input ?? {};[39m
[31m+         return getLeaderCandidateHistoryPage({[39m
[31m+           ...backtestOptions,[39m
[31m+           page: page ?? 1,[39m
[31m+           pageSize: pageSize ?? DEFAULT_LEADER_CANDIDATE_HISTORY_PAGE_SIZE,[39m
[31m+           phase: phase ?? null,[39m
[31m+           onlySuccess: onlySuccess ?? null,[39m
[31m+         });[39m
[31m+       }),[39m
[31m+[39m

[31m+           toName: z.string(),[39m

[31m+       )[39m
[31m+       .mutation(async ({ ctx, input }) => {[39m
[31m+         if (ctx.user.role !== "admin") {[39m
[31m+           throw new TRPCError({[39m
[31m+             code: "FORBIDDEN",[39m
[31m+             message: "仅管理员可校正股票名称与代码",[39m
[31m+           });[39m
[31m+         }[39m
[31m+         const result = await correctLimitUpStockIdentity(input);[39m
[31m+         if (!result.ok) {[39m
[31m+           const details = result.conflicts[39m
[31m+             .map([39m
[31m+               conflict =>[39m
[31m+                 `${conflict.limitUpDate}（已有 ${conflict.existingNames.join("、")}）`[39m
[31m+             )[39m
[31m+             .join("；");[39m
[31m+           throw new TRPCError({[39m
[31m+             code: "CONFLICT",[39m
[31m+             message: `校正会与以下涨停日期已有记录冲突，请先处理重复记录再重试：${details}`,[39m
[31m+           });[39m
[31m+         }[39m
[31m+         invalidateStockPriceSyncCache();[39m
[31m+         return { updatedRows: result.updatedRows, dates: result.dates };[39m
[31m+       }),[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     // 读取已记录的停牌窗口（供页面展示与人工撤销）[39m
[31m+     listSuspensionWindows: publicProcedure.query(async () => {[39m
[31m+       return await getStockSuspensionWindows();[39m
[31m+     }),[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     // 用 Tushare 个股日线反推停牌窗口并落库（管理员）[39m
[31m+     inferStockSuspension: protectedProcedure[39m
[31m+       .input([39m
[31m+         z.object({[39m
[31m+           stockCodes: z.array(z.string()).min(1),[39m
[31m+           startDate: z.string().regex(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/),[39m
[31m+           endDate: z.string().regex(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/),[39m
[31m+         })[39m
[31m+       )[39m
[31m+       .mutation(async ({ ctx, input }) => {[39m
[31m+         if (ctx.user.role !== "admin") {[39m
[31m+           throw new TRPCError({[39m
[31m+             code: "FORBIDDEN",[39m
[31m+             message: "仅管理员可反推停牌窗口",[39m
[31m+           });[39m
[31m+         }[39m
[31m+         const inferred = await inferStockSuspensionWindows([39m
[31m+           input.stockCodes,[39m
[31m+           input.startDate,[39m
[31m+           input.endDate[39m
[31m+         );[39m
[31m+         invalidateStockPriceSyncCache();[39m
[31m+         return inferred;[39m
[31m+       }),[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     // 人工标记停牌区间（管理员，兜底推断不可靠的情况）[39m
[31m+     markStockSuspension: protectedProcedure[39m
[31m+       .input([39m
[31m+         z.object({[39m
[31m+           stockCode: z.string(),[39m
[31m+           startDate: z.string().regex(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/),[39m
[31m+           endDate: z.string().regex(/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/),[39m
[31m+           note: z.string().optional(),[39m
[31m+         })[39m
[31m+       )[39m
[31m+       .mutation(async ({ ctx, input }) => {[39m
[31m+         if (ctx.user.role !== "admin") {[39m
[31m+           throw new TRPCError({[39m
[31m+             code: "FORBIDDEN",[39m
[31m+             message: "仅管理员可标记停牌区间",[39m
[31m+           });[39m
[31m+         }[39m
[31m+         if (input.startDate > input.endDate) {[39m
[31m+           throw new TRPCError({[39m
[31m+             code: "BAD_REQUEST",[39m
[31m+             message: "停牌起始日期不能晚于结束日期",[39m
[31m+           });[39m
[31m+         }[39m

[31m+           },[39m
[31m+         ]);[39m
[31m+         invalidateStockPriceSyncCache();[39m
[31m+         return await getStockSuspensionWindows([stockCode]);[39m
[31m+       }),[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     // 删除停牌窗口（管理员，撤销误标或已复牌的窗口）[39m

[31m+       .input(z.object({ id: z.number().int().positive() }))[39m

[31m[1m[7m FAIL [27m[22m[39m tests/server/research/parameterSearch/parameterSearchEffectiveness.test.ts[2m > [22mPARAMETER-002 §10（N-05）— 生产路径的 Parameter Space 派生器收敛状态[2m > [22m旧派生器必须带 `LEGACY / PREVIEW` 标记并写明「谁还在用」
[31m[1mAssertionError[22m: expected '/**\n * 由策略文档的 `parameters` 派生**参数搜索空…' to contain 'LEGACY / PREVIEW'[39m

[32m- Expected[39m
[31m+ Received[39m

[32m- LEGACY / PREVIEW[39m
[31m+ /**[39m
[31m+  * 由策略文档的 `parameters` 派生**参数搜索空间**（STEP B 落点③ 的接线件）。[39m
[31m+  *[39m
[31m+  * ## 为什么必须从文档派生[39m
[31m+  *[39m
[31m+  * `paramSearchRouter.ts` 的 `MAPPABLE_PARAMETER_DICTIONARY` 是一张**固定 8 字段白名单**，[39m
[31m+  * 未收录的维度落到 `switch` 的 `default: break` ⇒ **静默忽略**（P0-2 的直接证据：[39m
[31m+  * 调用方以为某维度参与了寻优，实际被丢掉且无任何提示）。[39m
[31m+  * 文档的 `parameters` 才是「这个策略到底暴露了哪些可调参数」的唯一权威声明，[39m
[31m+  * 因此搜索空间必须由它派生，而不是由调用方传一张字典。[39m
[31m+  *[39m
[31m+  * ## 三类参数的处理口径（**禁静默丢弃**）[39m
[31m+  *[39m
[31m+  * | 情况 | 处理 |[39m
[31m+  * |---|---|[39m
[31m+  * | `number` 且 `min` / `max` / `step(>0)` 齐备且 `min <= max` | 进搜索空间 |[39m
[31m+  * | 其余（`string` / `boolean` / 缺界或界非法的 `number`） | **如实记入 `excluded`**（带原因） |[39m
[31m+  *[39m
[31m+  * ⚠️ 为什么缺界时**不猜**：`min` / `max` / `step` 是搜索空间的**定义**，不是可以补的默认值。[39m
[31m+  * 猜 `step = 1` 会把一个 `[0, 0.3]` 的连续阈值变成 31 个搜索点 —— 那是拿「我们的臆测」[39m
[31m+  * 替换「研究者声明的意图」。这与 `strategyCandidate/definitionBuild.ts:454` 的既有口径一致[39m
[31m+  * （「范围必须由草稿声明 —— 数值参数要同时给出 min 与 max，Promote 不会替你给搜索空间定界」）。[39m
[31m+  *[39m
[31m+  * 本函数**纯函数、零抛错**：返回结构化结果（含 `excluded`），[39m
[31m+  * 由调用方决定「一个可搜索参数都没有」时是否响亮失败。[39m
[31m+  */[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ import type { ParameterSpace, SweepNumberParameter } from "../parameterSpace";[39m
[31m+ import type { StrategyDocument } from "../strategySchema/types";[39m
[31m+ import type { ResearchParameterType } from "../types";[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ /** 未进搜索空间的参数（**必须**如实带出去，禁静默丢弃）。 */[39m
[31m+ export interface ExcludedParameter {[39m
[31m+   readonly name: string;[39m
[31m+   readonly type: ResearchParameterType;[39m
[31m+   /** 为什么不进（逐条可读，含缺哪些界）。 */[39m
[31m+   readonly reason: string;[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ export interface ParameterSpaceDerivation {[39m
[31m+   /** 可搜索空间（可能为空 —— 空不等于错，由调用方决定怎么处理）。 */[39m
[31m+   readonly space: ParameterSpace;[39m
[31m+   readonly excluded: readonly ExcludedParameter[];[39m
[31m+   /** 文档声明的全部参数名（含已排除者）——供「如实回显」与审计对账。 */[39m
[31m+   readonly declaredParameterNames: readonly string[];[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+ /**[39m
[31m+  * 从策略文档派生搜索空间。[39m
[31m+  *[39m
[31m+  * 参数顺序 = 文档声明顺序（**不排序**：顺序本身是研究者声明的一部分，[39m
[31m+  * 且网格生成按声明序做笛卡尔积，保持顺序即保持 `evaluatedSamples` 可追溯）。[39m
[31m+  */[39m
[31m+ export function deriveParameterSpaceFromDocument(document: StrategyDocument): ParameterSpaceDerivation {[39m
[31m+   const declared = document.parameters?.parameters ?? [];[39m
[31m+   const parameters: SweepNumberParameter[] = [];[39m
[31m+   const excluded: ExcludedParameter[] = [];[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   for (const parameter of declared) {[39m
[31m+     if (parameter.type !== "number") {[39m
[31m+       excluded.push({[39m
[31m+         name: parameter.name,[39m
[31m+         type: parameter.type,[39m
[31m+         reason: `type=${parameter.type}（当前搜索空间只表达数值参数；非数值维度不被静默转成数值）`,[39m
[31m+       });[39m
[31m+       continue;[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     const missing: string[] = [];[39m
[31m+     if (typeof parameter.min !== "number" || !Number.isFinite(parameter.min)) missing.push("min");[39m
[31m+     if (typeof parameter.max !== "number" || !Number.isFinite(parameter.max)) missing.push("max");[39m
[31m+     if (typeof parameter.step !== "number" || !Number.isFinite(parameter.step) || parameter.step <= 0) {[39m
[31m+       missing.push("step（必须 > 0 且有限）");[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+     if (missing.length > 0) {[39m
[31m+       excluded.push({[39m
[31m+         name: parameter.name,[39m
[31m+         type: parameter.type,[39m
[31m+         reason: `数值参数缺 ${missing.join(" / ")}——搜索空间的范围必须由文档声明，不替你猜`,[39m
[31m+       });[39m
[31m+       continue;[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     // 走到这里 min / max / step 已确认是有限数字；排除顺序倒挂（否则 validateParameterSpace 会抛）[39m
[31m+     if (parameter.min! > parameter.max!) {[39m
[31m+       excluded.push({[39m
[31m+         name: parameter.name,[39m
[31m+         type: parameter.type,[39m
[31m+         reason: `min(${parameter.min}) > max(${parameter.max})——范围倒挂，不替你交换`,[39m
[31m+       });[39m
[31m+       continue;[39m
[31m+     }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+     parameters.push({[39m
[31m+       type: "number",[39m
[31m+       name: parameter.name,[39m
[31m+       min: parameter.min!,[39m
[31m+       max: parameter.max!,[39m
[31m+       step: parameter.step!,[39m
[31m+     });[39m
[31m+   }[39m
[31m+[39m
[31m+   return {[39m
[31m+     space: { parameters },[39m
[31m+     excluded,[39m
[31m+     declaredParameterNames: declared.map(parameter => parameter.name),[39m
[31m+   };[39m
[31m+ }[39m
[31m+[39m
